In scope: identity stability across re-renders. Out of scope: initial publish behavior. | [L238](https://github.com/Zocdoc/frontend-monorepo/blob/2d037fbe7e954ea1bf195754667995c897bc44e8/webapps/patient-web-app/src/SearchAI/components/ChatWindow/__tests__/TriageChatWrapper-quickChipPath-test.tsx#L238) |
| 429 | publishes the raw producer callback and never reaches the controller when the chat-state v2 flag is off | TriageChatWrapper quick-chip path: with shouldUseChatStateV2 false, the published command is the raw producer callback and invoking it never touches the refinement controller, though it still sends the triage selection | Render <TriageChatWrapper shouldUseChatStateV2={false} onSelectOptionReady={spy} /> -> Settle initial stream and wait for the spy -> Spy on the controller's selectRefinement -> Invoke the published command with chip data inside act() -> Assert selectRefinement not called -> Assert sendTriageSelection called once with the expected values | Verifies the flag-off arm bypasses the refinement controller entirely while still completing the legacy wire call. | TriageChatWrapper quick-chip path (flag off).
